1. # **Qualifications, Resources and Experience**

Utica Security Services Inc. is a licensed, bonded, and fully insured security services firm in New York with extensive experience providing professional guard services to large, high-volume public facilities. We specialize in delivering reliable, compliant, and customer-focused security solutions tailored for parking facilities, construction locations, transportation hubs, retail and commercial businesses, and complex mixed-use properties that require continuous coverage, strong supervision, and public-facing professionalism. Founded on the core values of discipline, professionalism, and integrity, Utica Security Service, Inc prides itself on reliable staffing, compliant payroll, rigorous supervision, and advanced reporting technology. Our organization maintains a structured management team, trained supervisory staff, and a scalable workforce capable of supporting 24/7 operations across multiple locations. We place strong emphasis on regulatory compliance, workforce training, accountability, and proactive communication with our clients. These principles have enabled us to successfully support public and quasi-public entities where safety, consistency, and public trust are critical.

## **1.1 Statement of Interest**

Utica Security Service, Inc. is pleased to submit this technical proposal in response to Rochester Genesee Regional Transportation Authority (RGRTA) RFP – Security Guard Services (Event 365). We are highly interested in partnering with RGRTA to provide armed, unarmed, and supervisory security services that enhance safety, support transit operations, and promote a secure and welcoming environment for passengers, employees, and visitors.

We are particularly interested in this engagement due to the critical role security plays within public transportation systems. Utica Security Services Inc. understands the operational challenges associated with transit centers and public facilities, including high pedestrian volume, diverse populations, variable operating hours, and the need for rapid response, situational awareness, and effective de-escalation. Our officers are trained to operate within these environments while maintaining professionalism, compliance, and sound judgment.

Our approach emphasizes strong on-site supervision, clear communication with client representatives, and proactive issue resolution. We are committed to maintaining full staffing levels, providing immediate management support when needed, and working collaboratively with RGRTA to meet service expectations and performance objectives. Utica Security Services Inc. also recognizes the importance of transparency, accountability, and adherence to all applicable federal, New York State, and local regulations governing security services.

Our firm’s operating philosophy is fully aligned with RGRTA’s emphasis on visible deterrence, proactive engagement, de‑escalation, and customer‑focused security delivery. We approach transit security not as a passive guarding function, but as an integrated public‑facing safety service that supports operational continuity, rider confidence, and regulatory compliance.

Utica Security Services Inc. views this opportunity as a long-term partnership with RGRTA. We are confident that our experience, organizational capacity, and commitment to quality service position us to successfully support RGRTA’s safety and security goals. We welcome the opportunity to contribute to the protection of RGRTA facilities and to deliver dependable, professional security services in accordance with the standards outlined in this RFP. Utica Security Services Inc. is prepared to support RGRTA throughout the full five-year Event 365 contract term with scalable staffing, consistent supervision, and sustained compliance.

## **1.3 Firm Overview: Size, Structure, and Staffing Levels**

Utica Security Services Inc. is a New York State licensed and insured security services firm with over four years of operational experience delivering professional guard services to public, private, and quasi-government clients. The firm maintains a scalable workforce of trained security professionals capable of supporting continuous, multi-site operations, including high-visibility public environments such as transit facilities. The company maintains a workforce of over 100 licensed New York State security personnel, supported by 10 supervisors and 15 operations and administrative staff, allowing sustained 24/7 coverage across multiple public facilities.

The company’s size allows it to balance operational depth with management accessibility, enabling rapid deployment, consistent service delivery, and sustained performance over the full contract term. Utica Security Services maintains sufficient administrative, supervisory, and field resources to support 24/7 operations, emergency surge requirements, and special events without service disruption.

This capacity ensures the firm can meet RGRTA’s operational demands while maintaining high standards of safety, reliability, and passenger confidence.

**Years in Operation:** About five (5) years of continuous service in New York State

**Core Services:** Transit Security Operations, Advanced Unarmed Security Services, Site Supervision & 24/7 On-Call Management, Access Control & Patrol Services, Incident Response & De-Escalation, Staffing Coverage Integrity & Performance Oversight.

## **Staffing Levels and Operational Readiness**

Utica Security Services, Inc. maintains a robust and scalable staffing structure designed to ensure uninterrupted security coverage for high-volume public transit environments such as RGRTA facilities. Our organization employs a diversified workforce of licensed New York State security professionals, including advanced unarmed security guards, site supervisors, and dedicated operations management personnel. Staffing levels are intentionally maintained above baseline contract requirements to provide operational resilience, immediate coverage for absenteeism, and rapid response to emergent or expanded service needs. In addition to regularly assigned personnel, Utica Security maintains a pool of pre-vetted, trained, and deployment-ready backup staff and floaters to ensure all posts remain continuously filled in compliance with RGRTA’s staffing plan. This staffing capacity enables Utica Security to meet all required hours, sustain supervisory presence, and accommodate RGRTA-directed adjustments without service disruption, thereby minimizing operational risk and ensuring consistent, reliable performance.

Utica Security Services maintains staffing levels specifically structured to support continuous transit operations, including peak periods, service interruptions, and emergency conditions. The firm employs a balanced mix of advanced/certified unarmed guards and experienced supervisors, all trained in accordance with New York State requirements and transit-specific operational expectations.

To ensure uninterrupted coverage, the firm maintains:

* Dedicated site-based personnel for consistency and familiarity  
* Trained relief and float staff to cover absences, leave, or call-outs  
* On-call supervisory coverage for reminding, incidents, and emergencies

Staffing plans are developed to prevent fatigue, minimize overtime reliance, and ensure officers remain alert, professional, and responsive. Supervisory ratios are structured to provide active oversight, routine post inspections, and immediate response to escalate incidents.

This staffing model ensures RGRTA receives:

* Fully staffed posts at all times  
* Rapid response to emergent situations  
* Seamless continuity of service during operational disruptions

## **Commitment to Transit Authority Performance Standards**

Utica Security Services’ size, structure, and staffing approach are intentionally aligned to the operational realities of a public transportation environment. The firm’s organizational design and staffing depth support proactive security presence, effective incident management, regulatory compliance, and customer-focused service delivery.

Through disciplined management, structured supervision, and scalable staffing capacity, Utica Security Services is positioned to deliver consistent, high-quality security services that will enhance RGRTA's organisational strategic planning, upgrade service standards, and improve performance.

## **1.4 Experience in Large, High-Volume Public Facilities**

Utica Security Services, Inc. has about five (5) years of experience delivering security services in large, high-volume public facilities characterized by continuous public access, fluctuating activity levels, and heightened expectations for professionalism, safety, and customer interaction. These environments require a security posture that balances visible deterrence, proactive engagement, and rapid incident response without disrupting public operations.

Security service in high-volume public spaces demands not only vigilant risk mitigation but also seamless integration with facility operations. Our experience aligns directly with the operational realities of RGRTA facilities, including:

* Large daily pedestrian flows   
* Multiple fixed posts requiring continuous 24/7 staffing  
* Coordination with operations and emergency response functions  
* Non-secure, open public access  
* Extended operating hours with peak and off-peak demand  
* Diverse passenger populations, including vulnerable and distressed individuals  
* High-visibility customer engagement expectations  
* Integration with transit operations, supervisors, and external responders

Security staff assigned to these environments are trained and supervised in accordance with RGRTA’s Event 365 Scope of Work and the Security Officers and Patrol Services (IFPO) Competency Model, ensuring professionalism, reliability, and situational awareness in all public interactions. 

Officers are trained to balance vigilant security enforcement with positive public interaction, which is essential in facilities where passengers rely on efficient and safe movement through transit hubs and associated spaces. Below are some of the areas our experience will serve RGRTA.

RGRTA’s Transit Center and related facilities serve as critical public infrastructure, supporting thousands of customers daily within a confined and dynamic environment. The Scope of Work emphasizes customer-focused security, proactive engagement, and integration into a broader safety and emergency management framework.

Utica Security Services’ experience in comparable public facilities reflects this same operational complexity. Our officers are trained to:

* Maintain a visible, authoritative presence while remaining approachable  
* Manage crowd flow and congestion during peak periods  
* Enforce customer rules of conduct professionally and impartially  
* Support customer service objectives alongside safety responsibilities  
* Respond to incidents without escalating disruption to service continuity

This experience enables our personnel to function as stability agents within high-traffic public spaces, reinforcing safety while preserving a welcoming environment consistent with RGRTA’s expectations.

## **Case-Style Operational** 

***(Relevant Experience, Proven Performance)***  
In a recent engagement involving two large public facilities with continuous operations and high daily foot traffic, Utica Security Services implemented a staffing and supervision model comparable to the one proposed for RGRTA.

Key elements included:

* Peak-period scaling of officers and supervisors

## **Safety Management & Risk Mitigation**

***(Public Safety, Loss Prevention, Risk Control)***  
Utica Security Services Inc. demonstrates a mature and proactive approach to safety management in large, high-volume public facilities where continuous access, fluctuating passenger density, and diverse security risks require disciplined operational control.

Our safety management practices are designed to:

* Maintain a visible, deterrent security presence  
* Identify and mitigate risks before escalation  
* Support safe passenger movement during peak and off-peak hours  
* Reduce opportunities for disorder, theft, and unsafe behavior

Security personnel are deployed in accordance with a structured staffing model that scales by hour and location, ensuring that safety resources align with real-time operational demand rather than static staffing assumptions. Supervisory oversight, as defined in the Organizational Chart (Figure 1), ensures that safety protocols are actively enforced, audited, and adjusted as conditions change.

## **Customer Service & Public Interaction**

***(Passenger Experience, Professionalism, Public Trust)***  
Utica Security Services recognizes that in transit environments, security officers are often the most visible representatives of the system. Our experience in high-volume public facilities has reinforced the importance of balancing firm security enforcement with courteous, respectful, and customer-focused engagement.  
Officers assigned to large public facilities are trained to:

* Provide clear directions and passenger assistance  
* Communicate calmly and respectfully under stress  
* Support vulnerable individuals while maintaining authority  
* De-escalate tense interactions before they disrupt operations

This customer-service-oriented approach is supported by advanced and supervisory personnel positioned during peak periods (see *Staffing Model*), allowing frontline officers to escalate concerns appropriately without overreaction or delay.

## **Incident Response & De-Escalation**

***(Emergency Readiness, Response Effectiveness, Coordination)***  
Utica Security Services has direct experience responding to incidents in large, high-traffic public environments, including behavioral disturbances, medical emergencies, service disruptions, and safety threats.  
Our incident response framework emphasizes:

* Immediate on-scene control by trained officers  
* Rapid escalation to supervisors and advanced officers  
* Clear communication with operations and external responders  
* Preservation of safety and service continuity

The firm’s chain of command, as illustrated in *Organizational Chart*, ensures that incidents are managed through clearly defined authority lines, reducing confusion and response time. Advanced Security Officers and Supervisors are deployed strategically to serve as incident leads until law enforcement or emergency services arrive, consistent with transit-authority incident management expectations.

## **Staffing Depth & Operational Resilience**

***(Continuity of Service, Reliability, Staffing Adequacy)***  
High-volume public facilities demand security operations that can absorb disruptions without service degradation. Utica Security Services’ experience includes operating in environments where absences, surges, and unexpected events are routine.  
To address this, the firm maintains:

* Relief and float staffing above minimum requirements  
* Cross-trained personnel for fixed and roving posts  
* On-call supervisory and advanced officer coverage  
* Hour-by-hour staffing adjustments tied to facility activity

## **1.7 Government and Public Sector Clients**

Utica Security Services, Inc. has not previously served as the prime security contractor for a government agency or public transit authority. However, the firm has extensive experience supporting public-facing, highly regulated operating environments through its role as a major vendor to established security contracting firms. In these assignments, Utica Security personnel operated under strict client-mandated procedures, formal reporting requirements, and professional conduct standards comparable to those required in public-sector and transit settings. These contracts are facilitated by prime security like: 

* Protos Security Services  
* Retail Security services  
* St. Moritz SecurityServices Inc.  
* NESCTC security services  
* Alpha Security Solution

These engagements required compliance with written post orders, incident reporting standards, attendance verification, supervisory oversight, and client audits—operating conditions that closely mirror public-sector security requirements. Our management team is experienced in working under contracts where policies are non-negotiable, performance is continuously monitored, and corrective action is required without delay.  Utica Security Services understands that public-sector contracts demand heightened transparency, accountability, and responsiveness. We are fully prepared to operate under RGRTA’s oversight structure, comply with all reporting, training, and conduct standards, and adapt our internal controls to meet the expectations of a public transportation authority. The firm views this engagement as an opportunity to extend its proven operational discipline into a public-sector environment and is fully committed to meeting or exceeding RGRTA’s standards for professionalism, compliance, and public service.

## **1.9 Staff Roles and Responsibilities**

Utica Security Services, Inc. has established a clear, scalable, and fully accountable staffing structure dedicated to supporting the operational, safety, and customer service needs of the Rochester Genesee Regional Transportation Authority (RGRTA). Our staffing model is designed to ensure continuous coverage, rapid supervisory response, and strict compliance with RGRTA requirements and New York State regulations. At the executive level, the Chief Executive Officer (CEO) maintains ultimate contractual accountability to RGRTA, ensuring that performance standards, regulatory compliance, and service expectations are consistently met or exceeded. The Director of Operations provides centralized oversight of all RGRTA security operations, with direct responsibility for staffing adequacy, deployment integrity, and operational performance across all assigned facilities and transit assets. Day-to-day RGRTA operations are managed by an Operations Manager, supported by dedicated Site/Field Supervisors who provide 24/7 on-call management coverage. Supervisors serve as the immediate point of contact for incident response, attendance verification, post-order enforcement, and real-time coordination with RGRTA representatives. This structure ensures that issues are identified and corrected promptly, without disruption to transit operations. Frontline security services are delivered by licensed, trained Security Officers (advanced unarmed) who are specifically prepared for transit environments. Officers perform access control, patrols, passenger assistance, de-escalation, emergency response, and detailed reporting while maintaining a visible, professional presence that enhances rider safety and public confidence. To protect coverage integrity, Utica Security maintains dedicated recruitment, training, and deployment functions. The Human Resources Manager and Recruitment/Discharge Cells ensure that only fully vetted, licensed, and qualified personnel are assigned to RGRTA, while maintaining a ready pipeline of replacement staff to address absenteeism or surge requirements. The Training Manager oversees all RGRTA-aligned initial, on-the-job, and annual in-service training to ensure continuous compliance and skill development. Administrative, logistics, and financial support functions—including deployment coordination, equipment management, payroll, and reporting—operate in parallel to frontline operations to ensure officers are properly equipped, compensated on time, and supported without distraction from their core safety responsibilities. This staffing structure provides RGRTA with clear lines of authority, uninterrupted coverage, rapid response capability, and full transparency, ensuring reliable security services that align with RGRTA’s operational mission, safety objectives, and public service standards.

| Role | Safety Management | Customer Service & Public Interaction | Oversight & Accountability | Compliance & Coverage Integrity |
| :---- | :---- | :---- | :---- | :---- |
| **Chief Executive Officer** | Establishes enterprise-wide safety expectations aligned with RGRTA policies and NY regulations. | Sets organizational tone emphasizing professionalism and public trust. | Holds ultimate accountability for contract performance and corrective action. | Ensures contractual, insurance, and regulatory compliance throughout the RGRTA engagement. |
| **Director of Operations** | Oversees incident response readiness, emergency protocols, and safety performance metrics. | Ensures service delivery aligns with RGRTA rider experience expectations. | Monitors supervisory performance and operational effectiveness. | Maintains staffing sufficiency, redundancy, and continuity across all RGRTA posts. |
| **Operations Manager** | Enforces post orders, emergency procedures, and safety compliance at the operational level. | Ensures officers provide respectful, consistent passenger assistance. | Directs supervisors and ensures immediate corrective actions. | Manages daily schedules, absenteeism mitigation, and relief coverage. |
| **Site / Field Supervisors (24/7 On-Call)** | Respond immediately to safety incidents and coordinate with emergency responders. | Intervene in escalated customer service situations to ensure professional resolution. | Conduct inspections, audits, and performance evaluations. | Verify attendance, initiate coverage replacements, and maintain real-time operational control. |
| **Security Officers (Advanced Unarmed Guard)** | Execute patrols, access control, de-escalation, and emergency response. | Serve as frontline representatives assisting passengers and staff. | Follow supervisory direction and reporting requirements. | Maintain assigned posts and comply with schedules and licensing requirements. |
| **Training Manager** | Ensures officers receive transit-specific safety and emergency training | Integrates customer service and de-escalation training into curricula. | Tracks certifications and training compliance | Guarantees training readiness before deployment to RGRTA sites. |
| **Human Resource Manager** | Oversees background checks, licensing, and fitness-for-duty standards. | Ensures staff professionalism through vetting and conduct standards. | Maintains personnel accountability records. | Prevents unqualified staffing and manages replacements without service gaps. |

## **1.10 Organizational Chart – Subcontractor Interaction**

Subcontractors are structurally isolated from RGRTA, reporting directly to Utica’s Director of Operations, with coordination handled by the Operations Manager to ensure seamless integration without disruption to service delivery. This preserves a single chain of command, eliminates fragmented authority, and ensures consistent enforcement of RGRTA post orders, training standards, and reporting protocols. The model supports scalability without compromising safety, professionalism, or compliance, positioning Utica Security Services, Inc. as a low-risk, highly controlled prime contractor capable of meeting RGRTA’s operational demands.

# **1.10  Subcontractor Coordination & Integration Outline**

## **Overall Control Principle**

Utica Security Services, Inc. will serve as the prime contractor, maintaining full operational, administrative, and contractual responsibility for all services delivered to RGRTA. Any subcontractor engagement will be supportive, limited in scope, and fully governed by Utica’s policies, procedures, and RGRTA requirements.

## **1\. Staffing & Personnel Augmentation**

**Objective:**  
 Ensure uninterrupted security coverage during peak demand, emergencies, or special events.  
**Utica Responsibilities**

* Determines staffing needs and authorizes subcontractor support  
* Verifies NYS licensing, certifications, and eligibility  
* Assigns posts, schedules, and supervision  
* Enforces RGRTA post orders and conduct standards

**Subcontractor Role**

* Supplies pre-approved, licensed personnel only upon request  
* Operates under Utica supervision and command  
* No direct communication with RGRTA

**RGRTA Benefit**

* Coverage continuity without loss of control or accountability

## **2\. Training & Specialized Instruction Support**

**Objective:**  
 Supplement internal training capacity with specialized or advanced instruction relevant to transit operations.  
**Utica Responsibilities**

* Defines training content aligned with RGRTA standards  
* Approves instructors and curricula  
* Maintains training records and compliance documentation  
* Authorizes deployment only after training completion

**Subcontractor Role**

* Provides approved training modules (e.g., de-escalation, crowd control)  
* No authority to deploy or certify staff independently

**RGRTA Benefit**

* Consistent, transit-appropriate training across all personnel

## **3\. Recruitment & Candidate Sourcing**

**Objective:**  
 Expand the recruitment pipeline while maintaining strict hiring standards.  
**Utica Responsibilities**

* Sets recruitment criteria and screening requirements  
* Conducts or validates background investigations  
* Makes final hiring and deployment decisions

**Subcontractor Role**

* Sources potential candidates  
* Submits candidates for Utica review only  
* No hiring or deployment authority

**RGRTA Benefit**

* Enhanced staffing capacity with uncompromised screening integrity

## **4\. Administrative & Compliance Support**

**Objective:**  
 Support documentation, onboarding, and compliance tracking without decentralizing control.  
**Utica Responsibilities**

* Maintains all personnel records  
* Oversees compliance with RGRTA and NYS requirements  
* Handles reporting, audits, and corrective actions

**Subcontractor Role**

* Provides supporting documentation as requested  
* Complies with Utica administrative protocols

**RGRTA Benefit**

* Single, consistent compliance framework

## **5\. Equipment, Uniforms & Logistics**

**Objective:**  
 Ensure uniform appearance, equipment readiness, and logistical consistency.  
**Utica Responsibilities**

* Establishes equipment and uniform standards  
* Approves issuance and branding  
* Oversees inventory and condition checks

**Subcontractor Role**

* Supplies or supports logistics only per Utica specifications  
* No independent issuance to deployed staff

**RGRTA Benefit**

## **6\. Emergency & Contingency Support**

 Provide rapid response capability during unexpected events or service disruptions.

* Directs all operational response

* Supports surge operations under Utica command

**RGRTA Benefit**

## **7\. Supervision, Reporting & Accountability**

**Utica Responsibilities**

* Manages incident reporting and corrective action  
* Retains full contractual liability

# **2\. Staffing Plan** 

## **2.1 Key Management Staff**

Utica Security Service, Inc. assigns experienced management personnel to oversee all aspects of staffing, supervision, and contract compliance. Key management staff possess demonstrated experience in security operations, workforce management, and public-facing environments.  
Key management responsibilities include:

* Contract oversight and client coordination  
* Staffing, scheduling, and supervision  
* Training compliance and documentation  
* Incident review and corrective action  
* Continuous performance improvement.

**Note: the resume of key management staff will be attached as a separate file during the submission of this document.**

## **2.2 Organizational Chart**

Utica Security Services operates under a clearly defined command-and-control structure designed to support public transportation security operations, regulatory compliance, and real-time incident management. The organization is led by the Chief Executive Officer, who is responsible for strategic oversight, contract compliance, and performance accountability. Day-to-day operations are managed by a dedicated operations leadership team that oversees scheduling, supervision, training coordination, and quality assurance. Field operations follow a structured chain of command that includes site supervisors, ensuring immediate on-scene authority and clear escalation pathways. Administrative and compliance functions are intentionally separated from field supervision to preserve oversight integrity, support the NYS Department of State's regulatory compliance, and ensure the consistent application of RGRTA policies and procedures.  
This structure provides RGRTA with:

* Clear accountability at every operational level  
* Rapid decision-making during incidents or service disruptions  
* Consistent enforcement of safety, conduct, and reporting standards

The organizational structure for this contract follows a clear chain of command.This structure ensures:

* Clear communication channels  
* Immediate supervisory availability  
* Rapid escalation and decision-making  
* Accountability at all staffing levels

## **2.3 Hiring**

Utica understands the comfort that comes from familiar working relationships and therefore organizes for continuity. Our benefit and compensation programs exceed industry standards, and we are confident in our ability to hire and retain staff who meet the established criteria for the contract and wish to remain with it. Apart from retaining the staff, Utica is committed to ensuring that RGRTA receives the best possible service. We understand that our success depends on the quality of our personnel, and therefore, we take great care in our recruitment process to ensure that we hire the most qualified individuals for our team. To hire for various positions required for our projects, we will use a multi-faceted approach to recruitment that includes traditional methods such as job postings, resume searches, and referrals, as well as leveraging modern tools such as social media, online job boards, and applicant tracking systems. We also actively reach out to candidates who possess the skills and experience that are needed for our projects. Our recruitment process includes a rigorous screening process that includes thorough background checks, drug testing, and personality assessments to ensure that our personnel are professional, reliable, and trustworthy. We also provide extensive training to ensure that our personnel are equipped with the necessary knowledge and skills to perform their duties to the best of their ability. Our recruitment approach will enable us to identify and hire the most qualified individuals to fill the positions required for our projects. Our staffing experts ensure that we are able to provide RGRTA with the best possible security services, delivered by highly trained and qualified personnel who are committed to delivering exceptional service.

**2.3.1 Hiring Process**  
Our Hiring Department is very efficient and skilled in hiring and recruiting qualified candidates for our clients. Our employees will be treated as a single labor pool from which all contract staffing requirements will be filled. We will select and assign the best-qualified individuals to all tasks, regardless of corporate affiliation. Our integrated and systematic 5-step Recruitment Process is used to identify, develop, and retain capable and skilled security personnel in line with current and projected business and client objectives:  

1. **Development of Position Description**

The hiring manager uses several job posting tools and resources, including site-specific screening questions and even security verification, to fulfill our client staffing needs. Through assigned recruiters, the department runs federal background checks from the past 4 years on all new hires, including the New York State Department of State Screening. This can also cover security officer registration affirmation with a state or agency commission. These checks and screenings ensure Utica Security hires only the most qualified and trustworthy individuals to protect our clients. Upon completion of the hiring process, the Hiring Department coordinates arrangements for the new hires’ training before their first day on the job site.    
Keys to successful staffing include the Proposed Project Team   
► Technical and managerial experience currently performing work that matches the SOW (Scope of Work).   
► Providing experienced Utica Security professionals and clients who work closely together in a collaborative, team-oriented environment. Identify, recruit, and screen exceptional, experienced employees locally. 

2. **“Pre-Qualified Workforce Deployment Model” Staffing Approach**

We adhere to this procedure and use established position descriptions to meet responsibilities and deliverable requirements.   
The stages are as follows:   
► **Stage 1** (Obtain Position Approval). The Operations Manager, based on examination of contract requirements, clarifies staffing needs with the Hiring Manager (agency customer concurrence is necessary to begin sourcing candidates).   
► **Stage 2** (Interviewing). This stage begins with the Director of Operations validating the program requirements and job description. We use internal resources and external resources (referrals, job boards, and online advertisements) to search for the most qualified candidates based on pre-screening qualifications and through assessing experience in meeting the requirements put forth in the job description.   
► **Stage 3** (Making an Offer). Based on team recommendations to hire, a contingent offer letter is presented to the candidate, then the Director of Operations discusses the candidate with the Chief Executive Officer(CEO) and presents the candidate’s resume for approval.   
► **Stage 4**: (Finalizing an Offer). After the CEO approval, final hiring is solidified, applicable background investigations and security clearances are obtained, and a start date is finalized. Candidates do not start until clearances are properly obtained and in place.   
► **Stage 5** (Employee Retention). We provide our clients with a stable, motivated workforce, which reduces turnover. Our Retention Plan focuses on communication, employee development, and benefits. It therefore cultivates an “Employer of Choice” atmosphere through competitive pay and benefits, investment in our employees, and operational success. 

3. **Recruitment Outreach**   
1. **Online** 

With a job posting notice developed, we review qualifications from those who recently worked for us. The recruiter then reviews resumes on websites.

2. **Social Media** 

Since most candidates seeking employment are actively engaged on various social media outlets, Utica Security using social media, we can engage with local and national security professional contacts regarding our available posts. One concern from job seekers is that they do not feel they are receiving timely feedback throughout the job search process. We notify candidates of any updates at each step. Because taking too long to decide could lead to candidates accepting other jobs, we provide timely communication with qualified candidates to keep them engaged in the recruitment process.  

3. **Referrals from Existing Employees** 

Referrals from our existing employees are often key to sourcing talent, especially if it comes to skilled positions. Tapping our employees’ existing networks extends the reach of our recruiting efforts, while maintaining the quality of candidates brought into the hiring process.  

**Employee Referral Bonuses** 

* Utica Security is pleased to offer a referral bonus program to qualified employees. Eligible employees are employees who have been employed by Utica Security for at least a hundred days and are neither in a managerial position or higher, nor working in the Hiring Departments. 

* Qualified employees may refer qualified persons for positions within the company and receive a VISA gift card for $100.00 (the “Referral Bonus”). To receive the Referral Bonus, the following requirements must be met: 

* The candidate for employment must list the Utica Security qualified employee on his/her employment application as the referring source. 

* The qualified employee must have attained eligibility before the date the candidate for employment receives an offer letter from Utica Security. 

* The qualified employee must not be a member of management and must not be assigned to the Hiring Departments. 

* The candidate for employment must be hired by Utica Security, pass all required background tests, alcohol, and drug tests, employment eligibility (I-9) verification, and meet all other conditions of employment. 

* The candidate for employment must remain employed by Utica Security for at least a hundred consecutive calendar days. 

* The candidate for employment must not be someone over whom the qualified employee has supervisory authority or who otherwise presents a conflict of interest. 

* The referral bonus will not be given to any otherwise qualified employee; doing so violates the Utica Security Code of Conduct. 

* Qualified employees should expect to receive their referral bonus gift card in the mail within two weeks of the date the referral candidate reaches their 100 days of employment with Utica Security. Utica Security looks forward to receiving many quality referrals from our existing employees as we continue to grow our business. 

4. **Organizational Partnerships** 

Utica Security’s recruiters work with Security Guard Training Agencies/Schools across New York to find recently trained or certified guards seeking to leverage their skills and knowledge in the security industry.

4. **Candidate Filtering** 

Once a position has been posted and advertised, candidates apply via the applicant tracking system, completing a short employment application to gather pertinent personal data, and by submitting a resume and cover letter indicating the desired position. Each candidate’s resume will be thoroughly screened against the position’s job description to determine if the applicant possesses the minimum qualifications. Basic initial screening for all candidates will include:  

* A fully completed job application, with a copy of the applicant’s resume    
* Names and contact information of three work-related references   
* State/Federal background check (fingerprint)    
* A face-to-face meeting to validate the applicant’s identity

Our recruiters and hiring managers will schedule an initial phone interview with qualified candidates to obtain information like availability, salary requirements, and other preliminary information to assist the company’s search committee with their review. At the conclusion of the phone interview, a determination shall be made as to whether the candidate should be shortlisted for the position. Utica Security seeks to establish a diverse candidate pool of no less than 3-5 qualified individuals to be shortlisted and interviewed for each position. Those employees selected for continued consideration will be contacted to schedule a time and date for a formal interview.

**Existing Database of Candidates**   
We have a database of security personnel in specialties that is continually updated by personal contact by the hiring team. Using the database, our recruiters do extensive canvassing, establishing available candidates with proper backgrounds in the security industry. Our recruiters keep accurate computerized notes as to professional interests, needs, training, and career goals. The result is an optimal match between client need, candidate skill, and desired career. To save money, businesses cut pay rates to improve the bottom line. Qualified employees are rejecting positions in favor of those paying more, resulting in a less qualified talent pool. In contrast, Utica Security adopts an aggressive approach to salary negotiation to offer candidates a competitive compensation package of direct pay and benefits commensurate with ability, allowing Utica Security to attract qualified talent.

5. **Formal Interviews**  

Utica Security utilizes the formal interview process to determine if a candidate is a good fit in terms of technical competence and culture. Our interviews comprise the following:  

*  Introductions of each panel member.    
*  Brief description of the role for which the applicant is being interviewed.    
*  Description of how the interview panel will conduct the interview.    
*  Candidate overview of experience, education, technical skill, goals, etc.    
*  Time for each panel member to provide questions at the conclusion of the interview.  
* Time for the interviewee to ask questions.    
* Contact by phone, informing the interviewee of the next step


6. **Candidate Selection/Offer** 

Once the interviews have been completed, the Utica Security hiring committee will meet to discuss the interviewees. Each committee member will be responsible for objectively assessing the extent to which each applicant met their selection criteria as outlined in the position description and shall score the applicants using an evaluation matrix tailored and developed specifically to the required qualifications of the position, and to our organizational needs. The hiring committee selects from the short-listed applicants using the listed criteria:  

* Identify the best candidate based on qualifications and technical expertise    
* Identify the best candidate to seamlessly integrate within the client’s culture 

Once a candidate is selected based on the criteria, Utica Security recruiters verify the candidate’s data and documentation by conducting background and reference checks to ensure the validity of all provided information. Final screening for all selected candidates will include:  

* Background checks.    
* Written or telephone reference checks.    
* Completed and signed written employment agreements, including the job description.    
* Job-specific qualifications. 

After successfully passing our background and reference checks, the candidate will be given an offer for the position outlining its description, competitive compensation package, and term of employment (if applicable). Using this approach, Utica Security shall be able to effectively recruit and source qualified advanced security guards and supervisors as requested. 

**Employment Suitability and Qualifications**   
Below are Utica Security’s basic requirements when we hire any security officer personnel. All new hires must undergo Pre-employment examinations that include a physical exam, drug test, mental health assessment, and background check. 

**Suitability** 

1. **Personal Effectiveness Competency**   
* Must be able to work effectively with diverse individuals by showing empathy, understanding behavior, communicating openly, building trust, and respecting different perspectives.  
* Must act ethically and fairly, take accountability for decisions and outcomes, and learn from mistakes while upholding professional standards.  
* Maintain composure, present a professional appearance, and demonstrate a positive, respectful attitude that reflects well on the organization.  
* Adapt quickly to change by embracing new ideas, applying creative problem-solving, and adjusting plans effectively in uncertain or evolving situations.  
* Consistently meet obligations, follow policies, and pay close attention to detail while actively pursuing continuous learning, skill development, and growth to adapt to changing work demands.

**2\.  Academic Competency** 

* Possess a high school diploma or equivalency.   
* Must be able to communicate clearly and confidently.  
* Must understand and can apply basic security principles to the security of RGRTA's specific structure, system, or process.   
* Must demonstrate ability to speak, read, and write in English. Must possess the ability to write accurate reports. Additionally, must comprehend detailed orders, printed instructions, and training materials. Must be proficient with mathematics, English literacy, reasoning, ability, and knowledge skills required to perform security officer job duties.    
* Demonstrates the ability to apply logical, inductive, and deductive reasoning to analyze information, identify relationships, and solve problems effectively. Capable of critically reviewing and synthesizing data, distinguishing fact from opinion, drawing sound conclusions even with incomplete information, and clearly communicating reasoning to others.


**3\.  Workplace Competency**

* Possess the ability to collaborate effectively by understanding roles, building positive relationships, communicating openly, contributing to shared goals, and resolving conflicts constructively.  
* Plan, prioritize, and manage tasks and resources efficiently to meet deadlines, handle multiple projects, and adapt plans to changing needs.  
* Apply creative, big-picture thinking to reframe problems, integrate diverse ideas, leverage resources and technology, and develop innovative solutions that add value.  
* Identify and analyze problems, gather and evaluate information, select sound solutions, and implement decisions effectively while assessing outcomes and communicating results.


**4\. Technical Security Competency**

* Apply site access rules by verifying identification and authorization, granting or denying entry appropriately, operating access control equipment, documenting access, and clearly explaining access requirements to site users.   
* Conduct preliminary security investigations by identifying incidents, collecting and preserving evidence, using surveillance and interviews, and documenting findings to support loss control and legal or organizational actions.  
* Accurately document investigations using proper report-writing techniques, preserve evidential information, follow site procedures, and conduct effective handover briefings.  
* Secure and preserve crime scenes, control access, collect evidence properly, and conduct effective, policy-compliant searches for people or property.  
* Enforce site rules and laws through professional presence, lawful intervention, effective de-escalation, appropriate use of force, and safe, compliant handling of authorized weapons.  
* Conduct physical and electronic patrols, operate surveillance and security systems, monitor site activity, identify threats or incidents, and maintain a visible presence to protect assets and ensure timely intervention.  
* Respond effectively to alarms and emergencies by following site plans, coordinating with responders, managing scenes and evacuations, communicating clearly, and supporting recovery and continuity of operations.  
* Deliver professional, respectful, and bias-free service by communicating courteously, solving problems proactively, representing management positively, and upholding human rights while supporting organizational success.


**5\. Industry-Sector Functional Areas**  

* Provide professional security services through a specialized firm that assigns trained officers to meet client needs, offering diverse roles and opportunities for advancement through training and experience.  
* Prevent and reduce losses by enforcing policies, protecting assets and systems, conducting investigations, and delivering strong customer service through trained in-house or contracted officers.  
* Provide specialized, risk-based security to protect high-profile or at-risk individuals through advanced planning, threat mitigation, and close-protection measures.  
* Support emergency incidents by assisting with crowd control, lifesaving actions, and site-specific evacuation and response procedures to protect people and property.  
* Provide secure transport of cash and valuables using armored vehicles and trained guards designed to resist robbery, attacks, and extreme conditions.

**6\. Safe workplace competency**

* A. Ensure personal and workplace safety by anticipating hazards, preventing injuries, and complying with all health, safety, and regulatory requirements.  
* B. Follow emergency procedures, maintain a safe and clean work environment, provide first aid/CPR, and handle hazardous materials properly.  
* C. Participate in safety training, follow safe work procedures, use equipment and PPE correctly, and understand the personal impact of workplace risks.  
* D. Understands workplace safety rights, promptly reports hazards and incidents, and actively contributes to improving workplace safety.

**Physical and Mental Qualifications** 

1. **Physical Qualifications** 

All security officers will successfully pass a physical examination administered by a licensed physician. The examination will be designed to measure the individual’s physical ability to perform assigned security job duties: 

**Vision**   
a. Security officers with distant visual acuity in each eye will be correctable to 20/30 (Snellen or equivalent) in the better eye and 20/40 in the other eye with eyeglasses or contact lenses. Additionally, security officers must be free of color blindness and able to distinguish colors used in visual displays or badges, specifically red, green, blue, and yellow. 

**Hearing**   
a. Security officers will have no hearing loss in the better ear greater than 30 decibels average at 500 Hz, 1,000 Hz, and 2,000 Hz, with no level greater than 40 decibels at any one frequency (by ISO 389 “Standard Reference Zero for the Calibration of Pure tone Audiometer.” Must be capable of hearing a normal conversation 15 feet away.   

**Disease**   
a. Security officers will have no established medical history or medical diagnosis of epilepsy or diabetes, or, where such a condition exists, the individual will provide medical evidence that the condition can be controlled with proper medication so that the individual will not lapse into a coma or unconscious state while performing assigned security job duties.   
b. Officers must be free of any communicable diseases.   
c. Officers must be screened for Tuberculosis and be free of the disease before working their first shift and every two years thereafter.   
d. Utica Security will guarantee that the Unarmed Guards they deploy carry their medication in the prescription container with the pharmacist's label attached, which includes the pharmacy name, prescription number, doctor's name, drug name, and control number.   
e. Utica Security will not assign any personnel for duty if they are under the influence of medication that could hinder their performance in carrying a firearm or fulfilling their job obligations. 

**Physical Ability**   
a. Security officers will be in good health, without physical defects or abnormalities that would interfere with the performance of duties. They shall be physically capable of completing all assigned duties and work with reasonable accommodations as required to maintain a Security Guard License. They shall be able to do frequent and prolonged walking, standing, sitting, and stooping, and also have basic physical stamina in all its forms (i.e., endurance, temperature/climate, etc.). Each security officer is required to provide a medical statement completed by a healthcare provider proving the individual is “fit for duty.”  

2. **Mental Qualifications** 

Individuals whose security tasks and job duties are directly associated with the effective implementation of the licensee physical security and contingency plans will demonstrate mental alertness and the capability to exercise good judgment, emotionally stable, implement instructions, assimilate assigned security tasks, and possess the acuity of senses and ability of expression sufficient to permit accurate communication by written, spoken, audible, visible, or other signals required by assigned job duties.

**Drug and Alcohol Use**   
a. Security officers will be free of habitual alcoholism or illegal drug use.   
b. Security officers shall be tested with a urinalysis for the detection of controlled substances. 

**Drug Free Work Policy**   
Utica Security maintains a drug-free policy. All security officers employed are to go through a background process that includes drug testing. Employees are prohibited from reporting to work or working while using illegal or unauthorized substances. If a security officer is suspected of being under the influence, Utica Security holds the right to request a mandatory drug test at our expense.

**2.3.2 Background Check**   
Utica Security Services, Inc. conducts comprehensive background investigations for all security staff assigned to RGRTA, at the Vendor’s expense, in full compliance with applicable federal and New York State laws and RGRTA contract requirements. Prior to assignment, all personnel undergo identity verification, employment and reference checks, criminal history review (including out-of-state records where applicable), credit review, fingerprint-based criminal background checks, and verification of legal authorization to work in the United States. Screening protocols include Idemia(Identogo) or Special Agreement Checks (SAC) or National Agency Checks with Written Inquiries (NACI), meeting Tier 1 background investigation standards where required. Personnel are cleared for duty only upon favorable adjudication of all results; any adverse findings are reviewed and resolved in accordance with due process and regulatory requirements. Utica Security maintains complete background screening records for each employee and ensures that all personnel assigned to this contract possess a valid New York State Security Guard License.

1. **Criminal History and Background** 

a. Free of outstanding arrest warrants, pending felony or misdemeanor charges, no felony convictions, and no probation/jail time or deferred adjudication.   
b. A comprehensive Pre-Employment check will be conducted for every security officer candidate. Checks will be maintained by Utica Security to determine the suitability of employment. Checks will include a ten (10) year employment background, a minimum of three (3) personal references, criminal conviction records from the places the candidate have lived over the past 7 years, and fingerprinting.

**2\. Legal Age**   
a. Security officer candidates must be 18 years of age or older. 

**3\. Work Authorization**   
a. Lawful resident of the United States and have appropriate work authorization.

**4\. License and Identification**   
a. Must possess a United States resident.   
b. Must possess a valid New York Identification.

**6\. Experience**   
a. Unarmed security officers must have a successful security guard experience demonstrating an ability to meet and deal with the general public and the ability to maintain control under stress; Any military service may be credited toward this experience. 

### **2.3.3 Deploy**

Approved personnel are deployed only after completing RGRTA-specific onboarding, including post orders, facility orientation, customer-service expectations, reporting protocols, and escalation procedures. Deployment decisions are made to ensure proper coverage, role fit, and compliance with RGRTA staffing requirements.

### **2.3.4 Supervision**

Once deployed, officers are actively supervised through on-site inspections, attendance verification, and real-time oversight by Site Supervisors and Operations Management. Supervisors provide immediate guidance, enforce post orders, and coordinate response to incidents to maintain service quality and compliance.

1. ### **Shift Start Accountability & Coverage Verification**

Site Supervisors verify officer attendance, punctuality, and assignment to approved posts at the start of each shift to ensure full coverage in accordance with RGRTA’s required service hours and staffing plan. This directly supports RGRTA’s requirement for continuous coverage and proactive absenteeism management.

### **2\. Post Order & Facility Compliance Review**

Supervisors ensure officers operate strictly in accordance with RGRTA post orders, facility rules, and customer-focused conduct standards applicable to open, non-secure public transit environments. This aligns with RGRTA’s Scope of Work emphasis on professionalism, customer service, and rule enforcement.

### **3\. Active Field Supervision & Inspections**

Supervisors conduct routine and unannounced post inspections to observe performance, professionalism, situational awareness, and public interaction. This reflects RGRTA’s expectation for active supervision and oversight rather than passive monitoring.

### **4\. Real-Time Operational Control & Guidance**

Supervisors provide immediate direction and corrective instruction as conditions change, service demands increase, or deficiencies are observed. This supports RGRTA’s requirement for responsive supervision and effective incident control in dynamic transit environments.

### **5\. Incident Oversight & Response Coordination**

During escalated incidents, Supervisors assume control of officer response and coordinate communication with Operations Management, RGRTA representatives, and external responders as required. This aligns with RGRTA’s Scope of Work expectations for incident response, escalation, and coordination.

### **6\. Attendance, Absenteeism & Relief Coordination**

Supervisors continuously monitor attendance and immediately initiate relief procedures through Operations Management when absences or late arrivals occur, ensuring uninterrupted service delivery. This directly supports RGRTA’s Staffing Plan requirements regarding absenteeism and coverage integrity.

### **7\. Documentation, Reporting & Transparency**

Supervisors ensure the timely completion of incident reports, inspection logs, and attendance records in accordance with RGRTA reporting and oversight expectations. This supports RGRTA’s emphasis on accountability, transparency, and auditable performance.

### **8\. Escalation, Corrective Action & Follow-Up**

Performance issues or repeated deficiencies are escalated to Operations Management, and corrective actions are tracked through resolution to prevent recurrence. This aligns with RGRTA’s expectation for enforceable supervision and continuous improvement.

### **2.3.5 Improve**

Performance is continuously reviewed through incident reports, supervisor observations, training records, and performance metrics. Identified gaps are addressed through corrective action, retraining, or staffing adjustments, ensuring continuous improvement and alignment with RGRTA’s operational and service standards.

**2.4 Staffing Confirmation Process for RGRTA**  
Utica Security Services, Inc. employs a structured staffing lifecycle to ensure that only qualified, compliant, and properly supervised personnel are assigned to RGRTA facilities. The staffing lifecycle for the RGRTA Security Guard Services (Event 365\) contract includes:

**→ (1) NYS DOS Licensing Verification**  
 Verification of valid New York State Department of State security guard licensing and required certifications before consideration for assignment.

**→(2) Background and Drug Screening**  
 Completion of background checks and pre-assignment drug testing in accordance with RGRTA requirements, federal regulations, and New York State labor laws.

**→ (3) RGRTA-Specific Onboarding and Post Orders**  
 Contract-specific orientation covering RGRTA policies, facility rules, customer-service expectations, post orders, reporting procedures, and escalation protocols.

**→ (4) Field Supervision and Audit**  
 Active supervision by Site Supervisors and Operations Management, including attendance verification, post inspections, performance observation, and incident oversight.

**→ (5) Ongoing Training and Performance Monitoring**  
 Continuous training, refresher instruction, and performance monitoring to maintain compliance, reinforce standards, and support continuous improvement throughout the contract term.

## **2.5 Absenteeism and Coverage Management Plan**

Utica Security Service, Inc. ensures operational shift management that maintains uninterrupted security coverage while complying with all contractual requirements and labor regulations.

### **Duty Until Properly Relieved**

Security staff assigned to RGRTA facilities shall remain on duty until the end of their scheduled shift or until properly relieved by a qualified replacement.

### **Maximum Shift Duration Compliance**

Utica Security Service, Inc. strictly enforces the maximum shift duration requirement:

* **16-Hour Maximum Limit**: No security staff will work more than sixteen (16) hours in any twenty-four (24) hour period  
* **Exception Protocol**: The 16-hour limit may be exceeded only in the limited circumstance where a government-issued travel advisory or state of emergency restricts travel to/from RGRTA's facility and impedes a replacement from arriving. In such cases, RGRTA is immediately notified of the extended shift and the reason for the exception, and a replacement is deployed as soon as travel restrictions are lifted

### **Compliance with Agreed Staffing Plan**

Utica Security Service, Inc. commits to full compliance with the agreed-upon staffing plan that will be included in the Pricing Section of the contract:

* **Staffing Plan Adherence**: Security guard services are provided at the exact number of staff and hours detailed in the agreed-upon staffing plan  
* **Acknowledgment of Liquidated Damages**: Utica Security Service, Inc. acknowledges that failure to staff in compliance with the agreed-upon staffing plan may result in the assessment of Liquidated Damages pursuant to the contract to compensate RGRTA for redeploying its own employees to serve security roles

### **Accommodation of Staffing Plan Changes**

Utica Security Service, Inc. maintains flexibility to accommodate RGRTA-requested changes to the staffing plan:

* **30-Day Accommodation Commitment**: RGRTA may request changes to the staffing plan, and Utica Security Service, Inc. shall accommodate these changes within thirty (30) days unless granted a written extension by RGRTA  
* **Extension Requests**: If a 30-day accommodation is not feasible, Utica Security Service, Inc. will request a written extension from RGRTA with detailed justification and a proposed alternative timeline

### **Chronic Lateness and Absenteeism Management**

Utica Security Service, Inc. acknowledges that chronic lateness or absenteeism may result in RGRTA requesting removal of personnel from the Contract. Personnel removed for chronic lateness or absenteeism are replaced within one (1) week unless extended in writing by the Manager of Transit Center Operations or their designee.

## **2.6 On-Call Management Availability**

Utica Security Service, Inc. provides 24 hours per day, 7 days per week on-call management availability through a structured, two-tier escalation model that ensures immediate responsiveness to staffing, operational, and emergencies.

### **2.6.1 Two-Tier Escalation Structure**

**Tier 1 \* Site Supervisor (Primary On-Call):**  
A designated Site Supervisor serves as the primary on-call contact for RGRTA and on-duty security personnel, available 24/7/365. The Site Supervisor is responsible for immediate response to staffing deficiencies, routine incidents, and operational concerns, including on-site deployment when required. The Site Supervisor acknowledges all calls within **15 minutes** and initiates corrective actions without delay.

**Tier 2 \* Operations Manager (Secondary Escalation):**  
The Operations Manager serves as the secondary escalation authority, available 24/7/365 to address critical incidents, repeated staffing issues, policy deviations, or matters requiring management-level decision-making. The Operations Manager has full authority to deploy additional resources, authorize coverage adjustments, implement corrective actions, and coordinate directly with RGRTA leadership as needed. Escalation to Tier 2 occurs when issues cannot be resolved at Tier 1 or when critical situations require immediate management intervention.

### **2.6.2 Response Time Commitments**

* **Tier 1 (Site Supervisor)**: Acknowledges all calls within 15 minutes; responds to routine staffing and operational issues within 30 minutes; deploys on-site support within 2 hours for urgent matters  
* **Tier 2 (Operations Manager)**: Available for escalation immediately upon request; responds to critical incidents within 30 minutes; provides management-level decision-making and resource deployment as needed  
* **On-Site Deployment**: Site Supervisor or Operations Manager deploys to RGRTA facilities within 2 hours for critical staffing deficiencies or operational emergencies

### **2.6.3 Contact List and Maintenance**

Utica Security Service, Inc. provides RGRTA with a comprehensive contact list upon contract execution, identifying:

* **Site Supervisor (Primary Contact)**: Name, direct phone number (24/7 mobile), email address  
* **Operations Manager (Secondary Escalation)**: Name, direct phone number (24/7 mobile), email address  
* **Business Hours Administrative Contact**: Name, office phone, email address

**2.6.4 Contact List Update and Maintenance:**  
The contact list is maintained and updated as needed and/or upon request from RGRTA. Utica Security Service, Inc. will:

* Notify RGRTA immediately (within 24 hours) of any changes to designated contacts  
* Provide updated contact lists to RGRTA upon request  
* Conduct an annual review and update of contact information to ensure accuracy  
* Maintain a backup contact structure to ensure 24/7 availability is never compromised

### **2.6.5 Documentation and Logging Process**

All on-call interactions, escalations, responses, and resolutions are documented and logged to ensure accountability, traceability, and continuous improvement:

* **Call Logging**: All calls to on-call management are logged with date, time, caller identification, nature of issue, and response provided  
* **Response Documentation**: Each management response includes documentation of actions taken, timeline of resolution, and outcome  
* **Escalation Tracking**: All escalations from Tier 1 to Tier 2 are documented with the reason for escalation, actions taken at each tier, and final resolution  
* **Monthly Review**: On-call logs and response documentation are reviewed monthly by management to identify trends, assess response effectiveness, and implement process improvements  
* **RGRTA Reporting**: Summary reports of on-call activity, response times, and resolution outcomes are provided to RGRTA upon request

This structured approach ensures rapid resolution of issues, uninterrupted security coverage, and demonstrates Utica Security Service, Inc.'s commitment to 24/7 management accessibility and responsiveness as required by the RFP scoring criteria.

![][image2]

## **2.7 Remedy for Unfilled Shifts**

Utica Security Service, Inc. recognizes the critical importance of full staffing and has established a clear and enforceable remedy for unfilled shifts.

### **a. Notification Protocols**

* RGRTA will be notified immediately by e-mail or by Utica security management. upon identification of a potential or actual unfilled shift  
* Notifications will clearly document the reason for any staffing vacancy and the corrective actions initiated.   
*  Once coverage is restored, RGRTA will be promptly notified of the resolution, including the specific steps taken and the time at which the vacancy was fully rectified.

### **b. Use of Backup Personnel**

## **Backup Plans:** Recognizing the importance of contingency measures, Utica Security will establish robust backup plans to address any gaps in security coverage due to unforeseen circumstances such as illness or emergencies. Our backup plans will involve maintaining a pool of trained and qualified security personnel who can be deployed at short notice to fill in for absent guards. This proactive approach will minimize disruptions to RGRTA’s security operations and provide reassurance that their premises remain protected at all times. Utica Security is committed to delivering reliable and professional security services, and our overtime and backup plans reflect our dedication to ensuring the safety and security of RGRTA’s assets. 

Key elements include

* Advance scheduling with built-in relief coverage  
* Maintains on-call supervisors and backup security officers/floaters to respond to absenteeism or emergencies to ensure uninterrupted security services.  
* Real-time attendance tracking quickly sees your unassigned shifts in one view and plans accordingly  
* Immediate escalation of call-offs to supervisory and management staff.  
* Watch workers clock in and out in real time. Late clock-ins, off-sites, and no-shows all get flagged.  
* Deployment of pre-approved floater or reserve officers  
* Reassignment of supervisors to temporarily cover posts if necessary  
* Authorization of overtime to ensure coverage continuity

Supervisors are responsible for monitoring on-field attendance and confirming post-compliance in real time, while the Operations Manager oversees overall staffing readiness and promptly initiates coverage procedures as needed. This coordinated approach ensures continuity of service and prevents any disruption to RGRTA operations.

### **c. Timeline for Resolution**

* Coverage actions begin immediately upon notice of vacancy  
* The goal is to fill any unstaffed post within one (1) hour or less  
* Management oversight continues until full coverage is restored

### **d. Financial Remedy**

Utica Security Services, Inc. shall not invoice RGRTA for any hours not worked as a result of unfilled shifts. In the event of an error, oversight, or administrative discrepancy resulting in billing for an unworked hour, Utica Security Services, Inc. will reimburse RGRTA one hundred percent (100%) of the applicable hourly rate for each unfilled shift hour.

## **2.8 Staffing Capacity and Flexibility**

## Utica Security Services maintains staffing capacity above baseline requirements to ensure RGRTA experiences no disruption to coverage during call-outs, service disruptions, special events, or emergent conditions. We will staff to RGRTA’s approved staffing plan and hour-by-hour demand patterns, including weekday and weekend coverage requirements, and will scale resources during peak and high-risk periods as conditions require.

**Rapid Flex Capability**: The Operations Manager controls real-time dispatch of relief officers and float staff and can deploy replacements immediately, with Site Supervisors receiving and assigning relief officers on arrival.

**Change Readiness**: If RGRTA requests modifications to the staffing plan, Utica Security will implement the changes within 30 calendar days unless RGRTA grants a written extension. 

**Fatigue & Compliance Controls:** Scheduling rules are designed to prevent fatigue and ensure compliance with RGRTA limits, including no officer working more than sixteen (16) hours in 24 hours except under travel advisory/state of emergency conditions as permitted. 

**Surge Staffing:** For unexpected events (major incidents, severe weather, large service delays), Utica Security will activate its contingency pool and management escalation to increase visible supervision and restore staffing stability without compromising transit operations.

# **3\. Training Plan**

## **3.1 Training Philosophy and Objectives**

Utica Security Service, Inc. maintains a comprehensive training program designed to ensure that all security personnel assigned to RGRTA facilities are fully prepared to perform their duties safely, professionally, and in compliance with all contractual, regulatory, and operational requirements. Our training philosophy emphasizes:

* Safety and risk mitigation  
* Professional conduct and public interaction  
* De-escalation and conflict management  
* Regulatory compliance  
* Continuous skill development

Training is delivered through a combination of classroom instruction, scenario-based exercises, and site-specific orientation. Effective training programs fully-engage participants and generate enthusiasm for the work through:  
► Effectively imparting knowledge  
► Developing in-depth skills, directly translated to on-the-job performance.   
► Motivating participants to achieve the required goals.  
► Clearly conveying available corporate resources as well as where and how to employ them.

All these factors have led to the indicated turnover reduction, allowing us to provide a consistent, trained, and experienced workforce who show up on time, handle emergency situations as effectively as day-to-day duties, and are keenly aware of opportunities for service improvement that all our clients appreciate. Throughout the execution phase of the contract, Utica Security Service, Inc. will implement an extensive, but not obtrusive, training program that will keep our security officers up to date in the effective execution of their duties. This training will include all the Contractor Furnished training listed in the RFP at a minimum. In addition to the standard Training, and to assist Utica Security Service, Inc. management in tailoring project specific training effectively, Utica Security Service, Inc. will utilize a Quality Control Plan (QCP) that will consistently monitor security officer performance from a constructive perspective. This QCP activity provides Utica Security Service, Inc. management, as well as Client management, with ongoing data that indicates potential areas of necessary improvement to be concentrated on within Training Activities and Daily Supervision of the security officers. To facilitate this QCP, we will perform on-site Physical Performance Checks on a weekly basis for the first 3 months and then at random points thereafter. Utica Security Service, Inc. management will work closely with Client management throughout the Contract to develop a team effort approach for this Contract's execution. Reports and reporting methods will be mutually developed, and effectively maintained and utilized throughout the life of the Contract.

Security officers will be called upon to observe the physical environment for changes and suspicious behavior. This objective is typically achieved by conducting in-person or remote surveillance of the physical environment. Security officers are expected to notice and monitor minor changes to make sound decisions when devising a plan of action. The trainer focuses on:

► Decision-making  
► Recognizing patterns (situational awareness)  
► Observing minor, yet critical details  
► Recognizing typicality and detecting anomalies  
► Improvising responses  
► Interpreting and adapting to events  
► Prioritizing actions  
► Observing and monitoring individuals, identifying, and responding to potential threats, different types of patrolling and loss prevention should be discussed.

**Emergency Training**  
Security officers may encounter emergency situations at a worksite. They will need to accurately identify the risk factors associated with fire threats, bomb threats, weapon emergencies, suspicious packages, and explosive devices, and will need to know how to respond appropriately. The trainer outlines the different risk factors a security officer must be familiar with and how to protect individuals and property associated with an assignment (i.e., contain, activate, and evacuate). In addition to emergency responses, the trainer includes an overview of the basic principles of prevention and safety.  
The trainer will also focus on describing and detailing the following emergency response procedures:

* Fire emergency response procedures  
* Bomb emergency response procedures  
* Weapon emergency response procedures  
* Suspicious package emergency response procedures  
* Explosive device emergency response procedures  
* Potential roles of a security officer in emergency situations  
* How to implement duty of care  
* Legal requirements  
* How to protect and secure a crime scene

## **3.2 Contract-Specific Curriculum Standards/hours**

No matter how up-to-date our technology or how impeccable our operations, if our security officers are not doing their work, it becomes meaningless, so training the perfect security officers customized to your needs is the most important duty for us. We have carefully reviewed your Requirements and Qualifications of security officer Personnel in the RGRTA Scope of Work and certify that all Utica Security Service, Inc.'s security officers will meet and exceed the RGRTA requirements with no exceptions. We will submit all the required documentation including physical and mental fitness, background check, and drug test.

The following sections provide an overview of our training curriculum. For comprehensive details on all training modules, instructional methodologies, scenario-based exercises, and complete topic coverage, please refer to the attached New Employee Training Program document, which contains the full curriculum including all 15 required contract-specific training topics, detailed instructional hours, and hands-on training components.

Utica Security Service, Inc. acknowledges that the contract-specific training program, as detailed in the attached New Employee Training Program and In-Service Training Program documents, must be reviewed and approved in writing by RGRTA prior to contract execution. Upon approval, the training program will be incorporated into the final contract. Any amendments or modifications to the training program during the course of the contract must be approved by RGRTA in writing before implementation.
